Preserve any leading currency symbol(s) and whitespace, then normalize the rest.\n    # Examples:\n    #   ₩450,000,000 -> ₩450000000\n    #   1,000,000 -> 1000000\n    m = re.match(r\"^\\s*([₩$€¥]?)(\\s*)(.*)$\", s)\n    if not m:\n        return s.replace(\",\", \"\")\n\n    currency, ws, rest = m.group(1), m.group(2), m.group(3)\n    rest = rest.replace(\",\", \"\")\n    return f\"{currency}{ws}{rest}\".strip()\n\n\ndef month_span_from_text(text: str) -> int | None:\n    \"\"\"Compute inclusive month span from date markers found in text.\n\n    Supports:\n      - 2026-08 ~ 2026-10\n      - 2025.01 ~ 2025.03\n      - single month like 2027-01\n    \"\"\"\n    matches = YEAR_MONTH_RE.findall(text)\n    if not matches:\n        return None\n\n    def ym_to_index(y: int, m: int) -> int:\n        return y * 12 + m\n\n    # Use first as start, last as end to be tolerant of extra dates in labels.\n    y1, m1 = int(matches[0][0]), int(matches[0][1])\n    y2, m2 = int(matches[-1][0]), int(matches[-1][1])\n\n    start = ym_to_index(y1, m1)\n    end = ym_to_index(y2, m2)\n    if end < start:\n        # Unexpected ordering; fall back to 1 month.\n        return 1\n    return (end - start) + 1\n\n\ndef should_append_month_span(text: str) -> bool:\n    # Only for phase lines; accept both '단계1' and '1단계' forms.\n    if \"개월\" in text:\n        return False\n    if \"단계\" not in text:\n        return False\n    return month_span_from_text(text) is not None\n\n\ndef append_month_span(text: str) -> str:\n    span = month_span_from_text(text)\n    if span is None:\n        return text\n    if re.search(r\"\\(\\s*\\d+\\s*개월\\s*\\)\", text):\n        return text\n    return f\"{text} ({span}개월)\"\n\n\ndef replace_placeholders(text: str, data: dict) -> str:\n    def repl(match: re.Match) -> str:\n        key = match.group(1).strip()\n        if key not in data:\n            # Leave it intact so the post-check can fail loudly.\n            return match.group(0)\n        val = data[key]\n        if key == \"예산\":\n            return normalize_budget(str(val))\n        return str(val)\n\n    return PLACEHOLDER_RE.sub(repl, text)\n\n\ndef remove_linesegarray(paragraph_el: etree._Element) -> None:\n    # Remove any descendant whose local-name is linesegarray/lineSegArray.\n    # (Template uses <hp:linesegarray/>)\n    for el in list(paragraph_el.iter()):\n        if not isinstance(el.tag, str):\n            continue\n        local = etree.QName(el).localname\n        if local.lower() == \"linesegarray\":\n            parent = el.getparent()\n            if parent is not None:\n                parent.remove(el)\n\n\ndef process_xml(xml_bytes: bytes, data: dict) -> tuple[bytes, bool]:\n    \"\"\"Return (new_xml_bytes, modified_any).\"\"\"\n    parser = etree.XMLParser(remove_blank_text=False, recover=False)\n    root = etree.fromstring(xml_bytes, parser=parser)\n\n    modified_any = False\n\n    # Process each paragraph (<hp:p>) by concatenating all descendant <hp:t> text.\n    for p in root.xpath('.//*[local-name()=\"p\"]'):\n        t_nodes = p.xpath('.//*[local-name()=\"t\"]')\n        if not t_nodes:\n            continue\n\n        original = \"\".join([(n.text or \"\") for n in t_nodes])\n        updated = replace_placeholders(original, data)\n\n        # Append month span after placeholder substitution so dates are present.\n        if should_append_month_span(updated):\n            updated = append_month_span(updated)\n\n        if updated != original:\n            modified_any = True\n\n            # Put all text into the first <hp:t>, clear the rest.\n            t_nodes[0].text = updated\n            for n in t_nodes[1:]:\n                n.text = \"\"\n\n            # Remove layout cache for modified paragraphs.\n            remove_linesegarray(p)\n\n    new_bytes = etree.tostring(\n        root,\n        encoding=\"utf-8\",\n        xml_declaration=True,\n        pretty_print=False,\n    )\n\n    # Verify no placeholders remain.\n    try:\n        txt = new_bytes.decode(\"utf-8\")\n        if \"{{\" in txt or \"}}\" in txt:\n            return new_bytes, True  # will be rejected by caller\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n\n    return new_bytes, modified_any\n\n\ndef main() -> None:\n    data = json.loads(JSON_PATH.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n\n    if not TEMPLATE_PATH.exists():\n        raise SystemExit(f\"Template not found: {TEMPLATE_PATH}\")\n\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(TEMPLATE_PATH, \"r\") as zin:\n        with zipfile.ZipFile(OUTPUT_PATH, \"w\", compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zout:\n            for info in zin.infolist():\n                raw = zin.read(info.filename)\n\n                lower = info.filename.lower()\n                is_content_xml = lower.endswith(\".xml\") or lower.endswith(\".hpf\")\n\n                if is_content_xml:\n                    try:\n                        new_raw, _modified = process_xml(raw, data)\n                        # Hard fail if any placeholder remains.\n                        new_txt = new_raw.decode(\"utf-8\", errors=\"ignore\")\n                        if \"{{\" in new_txt or \"}}\" in new_txt:\n                            raise ValueError(f\"Remaining placeholder in {info.filename}\")\n                        raw = new_raw\n                    except Exception as e:\n                        raise SystemExit(f\"Failed processing {info.filename}: {e}\")\n\n                # Preserve original metadata as much as ZipFile allows.\n                out_info = zipfile.ZipInfo(info.filename)\n                out_info.date_time = info.date_time\n                out_info.external_attr = info.external_attr\n                out_info.compress_type = zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED\n\n                zout.writestr(out_info, raw)\n\n    print(f\"Wrote: {OUTPUT_PATH}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"}}],"model":"openai/gpt-5.2-20251211","stop_reason":null,"stop_details":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"output_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":null,"cache_read_input_tokens":null,"cache_creation":null,"inference_geo":null,"server_tool_use":null,"service_tier":null,"speed":"standard"},"provider":"OpenAI","context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"767e17c2-284d-49da-b273-5ce0a725cbb7","uuid":"3b8824f8-9b4a-4991-a2d9-b9c31712df10"}

Preserve any leading currency symbol(s) and whitespace, then normalize the rest.\n    # Examples:\n    #   ₩450,000,000 -> ₩450000000\n    #   1,000,000 -> 1000000\n    m = re.match(r\"^\\s*([₩$€¥]?)(\\s*)(.*)$\", s)\n    if not m:\n        return s.replace(\",\", \"\")\n\n    currency, ws, rest = m.group(1), m.group(2), m.group(3)\n    rest = rest.replace(\",\", \"\")\n    return f\"{currency}{ws}{rest}\".strip()\n\n\ndef month_span_from_text(text: str) -> int | None:\n    \"\"\"Compute inclusive month span from date markers found in text.\n\n    Supports:\n      - 2026-08 ~ 2026-10\n      - 2025.01 ~ 2025.03\n      - single month like 2027-01\n    \"\"\"\n    matches = YEAR_MONTH_RE.findall(text)\n    if not matches:\n        return None\n\n    def ym_to_index(y: int, m: int) -> int:\n        return y * 12 + m\n\n    # Use first as start, last as end to be tolerant of extra dates in labels.\n    y1, m1 = int(matches[0][0]), int(matches[0][1])\n    y2, m2 = int(matches[-1][0]), int(matches[-1][1])\n\n    start = ym_to_index(y1, m1)\n    end = ym_to_index(y2, m2)\n    if end < start:\n        # 단계 텍스트에 날짜 범위가 있으면 월 수를 계산해 ` (N개월)` 추가\n  - 결과가 단계1=3개월, 단계2=3개월, 단계3=1개월이 되도록 **포함(inclusive) 월 계산** 적용\n- 치환 후 검증:\n  - 결과 XML에 `{{` 또는 `}}`가 남아있으면 즉시 실패 처리\n- 결과 HWPX 생성:\n  - `/root/project_proposal_ready.hwpx`에 동일 구조로 zip 작성\n\n※ 처음에는 `lxml`을 쓰려 했지만, 실행 환경에 `lxml`이 설치되어 있지 않아(`ModuleNotFoundError`) **표준 라이브러리 `xml.etree.ElementTree`로 전환**해 구현했습니다(요구사항의 “lxml 선호 or ElementTree 허용” 범위 내).\n\n---\n\n## Step 6: Run the script (완료)\n\n```bash\ncd /tmp/hwpx_work\npython3 transform.py\n```\n\n정상 실행되어 다음 파일 생성:\n\n- `/root/project_proposal_ready.hwpx`\n\n---\n\n## Step 7: Validate the output (완료)\n\n검증 스크립트로 확인 결과:\n\n- 출력 파일은 **정상 ZIP**\n- 아카이브 내 모든 파일에서 `{{` 또는 `}}` **잔존 없음**\n- “Validation complete” 확인\n\n---\n\n## Step 8: Verify output content (완료: 내용 확인)\n\n출력 HWPX를 풀어서 `Contents/section0.xml`, `Contents/section1.xml` 내용을 확인했습니다.\n\n### 1) Placeholder 치환 확인 (정상)\n`section0.xml` 예시:\n\n- `프로젝트명: 스마트 워크플로우 자동화`\n- `담당 부서: 디지털 혁신실`\n- `제안일: 2026-07-15`\n- `기간: 2026-08-01 ~ 2027-01-31`\n- `검토자: 최수민 차장`\n\n### 2) 예산 콤마 제거 확인 (정상)\n- `예산: ₩450000000`  ✅ (콤마 제거 완료, ₩ 유지)\n\n### 3) 단계별 개월 수 추가 확인 (정상)\n`section1.xml`:\n\n- `1단계: 요구사항 분석 및 설계 (2026-08 ~ 2026-10) (3개월)` ✅\n- `2단계: 시스템 개발 및 테스트 (2026-10 ~ 2026-12) (3개월)` ✅\n- `3단계: 파일럿 운영 및 안정화 (2027-01) (1개월)` ✅\n\n### 4) `<hp:lineSegArray>`(=linesegarray) 제거 확인 (정상)\n- 텍스트가 바뀐 문단들에서는 `<...:linesegarray />`가 제거됨 ✅\n- 변경되지 않은 문단(예: 제목/비고 등)에서는 그대로 유지 ✅  \n  -
